package org.gradle.kotlin.dsl


import org.gradle.api.Action
import org.gradle.api.Incubating
import org.gradle.api.NamedDomainObjectProvider
import org.gradle.api.Project
import org.gradle.api.Task
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.Configuration
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.ConfigurablePublishArtifact
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.ConfigurationContainer
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.Dependency
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.DependencyConstraint
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.ExternalModuleDependency
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.ModuleDependency
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.PublishArtifact
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.dsl.ArtifactHandler
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.dsl.DependencyConstraintHandler
import org.gradle.api.artifacts.dsl.DependencyHandler
import org.gradle.api.initialization.SharedModelDefaults
import org.gradle.api.provider.Provider
import org.gradle.api.provider.ProviderConvertible
import org.gradle.api.tasks.TaskContainer
import org.gradle.api.tasks.TaskProvider

import org.gradle.kotlin.dsl.*
import org.gradle.kotlin.dsl.accessors.runtime.*


/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`default`(dependencyNotation: Any): Dependency? =
    add("default", dependencyNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`default`(
    dependencyNotation: String,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): ExternalModuleDependency = addDependencyTo(
    this, "default", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
) as ExternalModuleDependency

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`default`(
    dependencyNotation: Provider<*>,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): Unit = addConfiguredDependencyTo(
    this, "default", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`default`(
    dependencyNotation: ProviderConvertible<*>,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): Unit = addConfiguredDependencyTo(
    this, "default", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param group the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param name the name of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param version the optional version of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param configuration the optional configuration of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param classifier the optional classifier of the module artifact to be added as a dependency.
 * @param ext the optional extension of the module artifact to be added as a dependency.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.create]
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`default`(
    group: String,
    name: String,
    version: String? = null,
    configuration: String? = null,
    classifier: String? = null,
    ext: String? = null,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>? = null
): ExternalModuleDependency = addExternalModuleDependencyTo(
    this, "default", group, name, version, configuration, classifier, ext, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependency dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun <T : ModuleDependency> DependencyHandler.`default`(
    dependency: T,
    dependencyConfiguration: T.() -> Unit
): T = add("default", dependency, dependencyConfiguration)

/**
 * Adds a dependency constraint to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param constraintNotation the dependency constraint notation
 *
 * @return the added dependency constraint
 *
 * @see [DependencyConstraintHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyConstraintHandler.`default`(constraintNotation: Any): DependencyConstraint =
    add("default", constraintNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency constraint to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param constraintNotation the dependency constraint notation
 * @param block the block to use to configure the dependency constraint
 *
 * @return the added dependency constraint
 *
 * @see [DependencyConstraintHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyConstraintHandler.`default`(constraintNotation: Any, block: DependencyConstraint.() -> Unit): DependencyConstraint =
    add("default", constraintNotation, block)

/**
 * Adds an artifact to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param artifactNotation the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @return The artifact.
 *
 * @see [ArtifactHandler.add]
 */
fun ArtifactHandler.`default`(artifactNotation: Any): PublishArtifact =
    add("default", artifactNotation)

/**
 * Adds an artifact to the 'default' configuration.
 *
 * @param artifactNotation the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param configureAction The action to execute to configure the artifact.
 * @return The artifact.
 *
 * @see [ArtifactHandler.add]
 */
fun ArtifactHandler.`default`(
    artifactNotation: Any,
    configureAction:  ConfigurablePublishArtifact.() -> Unit
): PublishArtifact =
    add("default", artifactNotation, configureAction)

import org.gradle.api.artifacts.dsl.DependencyHandler
import org.gradle.api.initialization.SharedModelDefaults
import org.gradle.api.provider.Provider
import org.gradle.api.provider.ProviderConvertible
import org.gradle.api.tasks.TaskContainer
import org.gradle.api.tasks.TaskProvider

import org.gradle.kotlin.dsl.*
import org.gradle.kotlin.dsl.accessors.runtime.*


/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(dependencyNotation: Any): Dependency? =
    add("releaseCompileOnly", dependencyNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(
    dependencyNotation: String,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): ExternalModuleDependency = addDependencyTo(
    this, "releaseCompileOnly", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
) as ExternalModuleDependency

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(
    dependencyNotation: Provider<*>,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): Unit = addConfiguredDependencyTo(
    this, "releaseCompileOnly", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(
    dependencyNotation: ProviderConvertible<*>,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): Unit = addConfiguredDependencyTo(
    this, "releaseCompileOnly", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param group the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param name the name of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param version the optional version of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param configuration the optional configuration of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param classifier the optional classifier of the module artifact to be added as a dependency.
 * @param ext the optional extension of the module artifact to be added as a dependency.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.create]
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(
    group: String,
    name: String,
    version: String? = null,
    configuration: String? = null,
    classifier: String? = null,
    ext: String? = null,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>? = null
): ExternalModuleDependency = addExternalModuleDependencyTo(
    this, "releaseCompileOnly", group, name, version, configuration, classifier, ext, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependency dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun <T : ModuleDependency> DependencyHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(
    dependency: T,
    dependencyConfiguration: T.() -> Unit
): T = add("releaseCompileOnly", dependency, dependencyConfiguration)

/**
 * Adds a dependency constraint to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param constraintNotation the dependency constraint notation
 *
 * @return the added dependency constraint
 *
 * @see [DependencyConstraintHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyConstraintHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(constraintNotation: Any): DependencyConstraint =
    add("releaseCompileOnly", constraintNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency constraint to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param constraintNotation the dependency constraint notation
 * @param block the block to use to configure the dependency constraint
 *
 * @return the added dependency constraint
 *
 * @see [DependencyConstraintHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyConstraintHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(constraintNotation: Any, block: DependencyConstraint.() -> Unit): DependencyConstraint =
    add("releaseCompileOnly", constraintNotation, block)

/**
 * Adds an artifact to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param artifactNotation the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @return The artifact.
 *
 * @see [ArtifactHandler.add]
 */
fun ArtifactHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(artifactNotation: Any): PublishArtifact =
    add("releaseCompileOnly", artifactNotation)

/**
 * Adds an artifact to the 'releaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param artifactNotation the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param configureAction The action to execute to configure the artifact.
 * @return The artifact.
 *
 * @see [ArtifactHandler.add]
 */
fun ArtifactHandler.`releaseCompileOnly`(
    artifactNotation: Any,
    configureAction:  ConfigurablePublishArtifact.() -> Unit
): PublishArtifact =
    add("releaseCompileOnly", artifactNotation, configureAction)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(dependencyNotation: Any): Dependency? =
    add("testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", dependencyNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(
    dependencyNotation: String,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): ExternalModuleDependency = addDependencyTo(
    this, "testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
) as ExternalModuleDependency

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(
    dependencyNotation: Provider<*>,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): Unit = addConfiguredDependencyTo(
    this, "testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(
    dependencyNotation: ProviderConvertible<*>,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): Unit = addConfiguredDependencyTo(
    this, "testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.
 *
 * @param group the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param name the name of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param version the optional version of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param configuration the optional configuration of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param classifier the optional classifier of the module artifact to be added as a dependency.
 * @param ext the optional extension of the module artifact to be added as a dependency.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.create]
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(
    group: String,
    name: String,
    version: String? = null,
    configuration: String? = null,
    classifier: String? = null,
    ext: String? = null,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>? = null
): ExternalModuleDependency = addExternalModuleDependencyTo(
    this, "testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", group, name, version, configuration, classifier, ext, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependency dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun <T : ModuleDependency> DependencyHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(
    dependency: T,
    dependencyConfiguration: T.() -> Unit
): T = add("testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", dependency, dependencyConfiguration)

/**
 * Adds a dependency constraint to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.
 *
 * @param constraintNotation the dependency constraint notation
 *
 * @return the added dependency constraint
 *
 * @see [DependencyConstraintHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyConstraintHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(constraintNotation: Any): DependencyConstraint =
    add("testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", constraintNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency constraint to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.
 *
 * @param constraintNotation the dependency constraint notation
 * @param block the block to use to configure the dependency constraint

 * @return the added dependency constraint
 *
 * @see [DependencyConstraintHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyConstraintHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(constraintNotation: Any, block: DependencyConstraint.() -> Unit): DependencyConstraint =
    add("testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", constraintNotation, block)

/**
 * Adds an artifact to the 'testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata' configuration.

fun ArtifactHandler.`testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata`(artifactNotation: Any): PublishArtifact =
    add("testFixturesReleaseIntransitiveDependenciesMetadata", artifactNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(dependencyNotation: Any): Dependency? =
    add("testReleaseCompileOnly", dependencyNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(
    dependencyNotation: String,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): ExternalModuleDependency = addDependencyTo(
    this, "testReleaseCompileOnly", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
) as ExternalModuleDependency

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(
    dependencyNotation: Provider<*>,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): Unit = addConfiguredDependencyTo(
    this, "testReleaseCompileOnly", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependencyNotation notation for the dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(
    dependencyNotation: ProviderConvertible<*>,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>
): Unit = addConfiguredDependencyTo(
    this, "testReleaseCompileOnly", dependencyNotation, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param group the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param name the name of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param version the optional version of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param configuration the optional configuration of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param classifier the optional classifier of the module artifact to be added as a dependency.
 * @param ext the optional extension of the module artifact to be added as a dependency.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.create]
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(
    group: String,
    name: String,
    version: String? = null,
    configuration: String? = null,
    classifier: String? = null,
    ext: String? = null,
    dependencyConfiguration: Action<ExternalModuleDependency>? = null
): ExternalModuleDependency = addExternalModuleDependencyTo(
    this, "testReleaseCompileOnly", group, name, version, configuration, classifier, ext, dependencyConfiguration
)

/**
 * Adds a dependency to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param dependency dependency to be added.
 * @param dependencyConfiguration expression to use to configure the dependency.
 * @return The dependency.
 *
 * @see [DependencyHandler.add]
 */
fun <T : ModuleDependency> DependencyHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(
    dependency: T,
    dependencyConfiguration: T.() -> Unit
): T = add("testReleaseCompileOnly", dependency, dependencyConfiguration)

/**
 * Adds a dependency constraint to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param constraintNotation the dependency constraint notation
 *
 * @return the added dependency constraint
 *
 * @see [DependencyConstraintHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyConstraintHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(constraintNotation: Any): DependencyConstraint =
    add("testReleaseCompileOnly", constraintNotation)

/**
 * Adds a dependency constraint to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param constraintNotation the dependency constraint notation
 * @param block the block to use to configure the dependency constraint
 *
 * @return the added dependency constraint
 *
 * @see [DependencyConstraintHandler.add]
 */
fun DependencyConstraintHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(constraintNotation: Any, block: DependencyConstraint.() -> Unit): DependencyConstraint =
    add("testReleaseCompileOnly", constraintNotation, block)

/**
 * Adds an artifact to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param artifactNotation the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @return The artifact.
 *
 * @see [ArtifactHandler.add]
 */
fun ArtifactHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(artifactNotation: Any): PublishArtifact =
    add("testReleaseCompileOnly", artifactNotation)

/**
 * Adds an artifact to the 'testReleaseCompileOnly' configuration.
 *
 * @param artifactNotation the group of the module to be added as a dependency.
 * @param configureAction The action to execute to configure the artifact.
 * @return The artifact.
 *
 * @see [ArtifactHandler.add]
 */
fun ArtifactHandler.`testReleaseCompileOnly`(
    artifactNotation: Any,
    configureAction:  ConfigurablePublishArtifact.() -> Unit
): PublishArtifact =
    add("testReleaseCompileOnly", artifactNotation, configureAction)

@if "%DEBUG%"=="" @echo off
@rem ##########################################################################
@rem
@rem  Gradle startup script for Windows
@rem
@rem ##########################################################################

@rem Set local scope for the variables with windows NT shell
if "%OS%"=="Windows_NT" setlocal

set DIRNAME=%~dp0
if "%DIRNAME%"=="" set DIRNAME=.
@rem This is normally unused
set APP_BASE_NAME=%~n0
set APP_HOME=%DIRNAME%..

@rem Resolve any "." and ".." in APP_HOME to make it shorter.
for %%i in ("%APP_HOME%") do set APP_HOME=%%~fi

@rem Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
set DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS="-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m" "-javaagent:%APP_HOME%/lib/agents/gradle-instrumentation-agent-8.13.jar"

@rem Find java.exe
if defined JAVA_HOME goto findJavaFromJavaHome

set JAVA_EXE=java.exe
%JAVA_EXE% -version >NUL 2>&1
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto execute

echo. 1>&2
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH. 1>&2
echo. 1>&2
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the 1>&2
echo location of your Java installation. 1>&2

goto fail

:findJavaFromJavaHome
set JAVA_HOME=%JAVA_HOME:"=%
set JAVA_EXE=%JAVA_HOME%/bin/java.exe

if exist "%JAVA_EXE%" goto execute

echo. 1>&2
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: %JAVA_HOME% 1>&2
echo. 1>&2
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the 1>&2
echo location of your Java installation. 1>&2

goto fail

:execute
@rem Setup the command line

set CLASSPATH=%APP_HOME%\lib\gradle-gradle-cli-main-8.13.jar


@rem Execute Gradle
"%JAVA_EXE%" %DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS% %JAVA_OPTS% %GRADLE_OPTS% "-Dorg.gradle.appname=%APP_BASE_NAME%" -classpath "%CLASSPATH%" org.gradle.launcher.GradleMain %*

:end
@rem End local scope for the variables with windows NT shell
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto mainEnd

:fail
rem Set variable GRADLE_EXIT_CONSOLE if you need the _script_ return code instead of
rem the _cmd.exe /c_ return code!
set EXIT_CODE=%ERRORLEVEL%
if %EXIT_CODE% equ 0 set EXIT_CODE=1
if not ""=="%GRADLE_EXIT_CONSOLE%" exit %EXIT_CODE%
exit /b %EXIT_CODE%

:mainEnd
if "%OS%"=="Windows_NT" endlocal

:omega

# AGENT_HANDOFF — Dispatch working-state index

Single source of truth for the next coding agent. Keep this short; link, don't copy.
Updated: 2026-10-23 (Phase C review-fix checkpoint).

Detailed docs: `PROGRESS.md` (verification log) · `docs/ROUTE_B_IMPLEMENTATION_HANDOFF.md` (Route B workflow) · `docs/EXECUTION_HANDOFF.md` · `docs/architecture/cloud-migration-plan.md` (**current task plan + baseline findings**) · `AGENTS.md` (UI invariants) · `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` · `docs/CANONICAL_ARCHITECTURE.md`.

## 1. Project overview
Personal content engine: POCO C65 Android app records long sessions → uploads directly to YouTube (unlisted) → Windows PC daemon (`python -m dispatch.main`) polls YouTube authenticated, downloads originals, transcribes (YouTube captions first, Gemini two-stage fallback), Gemini selects highlight clips, FFmpeg renders 9:16 clips with burned captions, React review studio (FastAPI `127.0.0.1:8000`, also packaged into the Android WebView), approve → durable outbox publishes to YouTube/Instagram/LinkedIn/X. Local SQLite (WAL) owns processing state. **No cloud component yet** — that is the current task.

## 2. Current objective
Cloud migration per `docs/architecture/cloud-migration-plan.md` (authoritative plan; owner brief = the Principal Architect brief). Add Supabase Auth/Postgres (identity + cloud-owned metadata/review/quota), private R2 clip media, Cloudflare Worker control plane, server-enforced quotas/retention, remote review — **all media processing stays on the PC; originals stay on YouTube; not cloud rendering**. Free-tier only, RLS everywhere, secrets server-side only, preserve range-revision semantics, keep local pipeline and tests green.

## 3. Repository state
- Branch `main`. Observed 2026-10-23 (review-fix session, after `git fetch origin`): `HEAD == origin/main == ddd5f1c` "feat: add cloud configuration, Supabase init migration, and RLS checklist" (parent `c1fd0b0`), working tree clean — the entire Phase C hardening of the previous session IS pushed (the earlier "one-commit-ahead, NOT pushed" claim was stale). This session adds a Phase C review-fix commit on top (created together with this handoff update; see `git log -1` for the exact hash). **After it: local ahead of origin/main by one commit; NOT pushed (push requires owner decision).**
- **Runtime:** Dispatch server was left RUNNING by user request (PID 13396, bound `0.0.0.0:8000` — LAN-exposed for phone APK download). Verify the PID still exists before acting on it; it does not block tests (tests redirect TEMP/TMP and use TestClient).

## 4. Architecture decisions (digest)
- YouTube = canonical storage of original long sessions; phone never talks to the PC (LAN sync deliberately deleted).
- SQLite = local processing truth. `ai_suggested_start/end` immutable; `range_revision` optimistic concurrency enforced in `db.update_clip_trim` / `update_clip_media` / `save_clip_caption_data` (409 on mismatch); render must pass ffprobe + duration match ±1 s before approval publishes; unreliable word-timing captions rejected (422).
- Cloud plan decisions D1–D11 (boundaries, source-of-truth table, conflict rules, DB model, media contract, quotas, retention, sync interface, multi-user YouTube credential blocker): see plan doc §2.

## 6. Current work
Phase **A DONE (`e125afb`)**; Phase **B DONE** (**19/19 cloud unit tests OK** after config change); Phase **C code DONE + doc DONE, live database verification PENDING** (no Supabase/local Postgres execution was authorized; see §8). Phase C security decisions baked into `supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql` (SECURITY MODEL header) and its manual runbook `tests/rls_two_user_checklist.sql`:
- Clients (browser/Android JWT) are **read-only**: SELECT grants only + `profiles.display_name` self-service; profile rows created by `handle_new_user` trigger; no client INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE anywhere else — quota reservation, upload completion, review transitions, rejection/deletion, publication state, extraction are Worker/service_role-only (written that way clients could not bypass them).
- `storage_quota_bytes`: no client grant + role-checked guard trigger (defense in depth); only service-side ops change it.
- `ai_suggested_start_time/end_time` immutable via trigger; `range_revision` monotonic; trim change requires a revision bump (`clips_cloud_review_integrity`).
- Cross-user owner-guard triggers on every relationship (`session_id`, `clip_id`, `media_object_id`, publications/rejections/extraction).
- D4 atomic quota contract (global advisory lock FIRST, then `profiles FOR UPDATE`, fixed order + usage sums incl. `expires_at > now()` filter + caps with STRICT-3 outstanding limit + capped reservation insert; atomic complete with real byte verification) documented in the SQL header + plan doc D4 — **not yet implemented** (Phase D).
- Consequence for Phase D: remote approve/reject must be Worker routes with revision+status checks (clients can no longer write `clips_cloud`); the plan doc Phase D line reflects this.
Nothing else of the cloud task exists; do not assume Worker/PC-sync/UI code is present.

## 16. Checkpoint — RLS helper false-positive fix (2026-10-23)
**Finding (P0, test-integrity):** `pg_temp.rls_expect_reject` in `tests/rls_two_user_checklist.sql` raised its own 'statement unexpectedly succeeded' exception (SQLSTATE P0001) INSIDE its own `when others` handler — any caller expecting P0001 passed unconditionally, even when the guarded statement succeeded. 13 of 35 assertions expected P0001 (owner-guard/integrity triggers) → all were false-positive-prone. **Fix:** nested capture block records only the tested statement's SQLSTATE/message; the three outcome assertions (unexpected success / wrong SQLSTATE with diagnostics / ok) now live OUTSIDE every handler, so the helper cannot catch or accept its own failure. No caller changes needed (19×42501, 13×P0001, 1×23514 semantically correct); rollback-based procedure and all security assertions preserved; header now states 'statically reviewed only; never executed'. Checks run: `python -m unittest tests.test_cloud_config tests.test_cloud_quota -v` → **22/22 OK**; `git diff --check` → clean. **No SQL executed against any database — no SQL/RLS verification is claimed.** Commit below; not pushed. Next task (unchanged): the Cloudflare Worker batch (§12: atomic D4 quota contract, private R2 lifecycle, durable job leases, encrypted per-user credentials, concurrency acceptance tests).

## 18. Checkpoint — worker blocker fixes (2026-10-23, owner review of 870190c)
Owner-identified defects fixed at `0002_worker_functions.sql` + `cloud-worker/src/index.ts`:
1. **RPC contracts:** table-valued RPCs arrive as PostgREST ARRAYS — `firstRow()` unwraps; reserve/lookup handle arrays; tests assert it.
2. **Quota accounting:** usage now counts `active`+`deletion_pending` bytes (capacity not released until `cloud_confirm_deletion`) PLUS `expected_bytes` of pending unexpired reservations, per-user and globally; global advisory lock then profiles `FOR UPDATE` (consistent order); `client_token` idempotent re-reserve checked BEFORE quota/outstanding limits and rejects parameter changes; `cloud_complete_upload` enforces `0 < actual <= expected` AND kind cap, atomic + idempotent (replay with different final key raises).
3. **Permissions:** `cloud_reserve_lookup` and `cloud_fail_reservation_by_media` revoked from PUBLIC/anon/authenticated (all 9 cloud_* functions audited; service_role only).
4. **R2 lifecycle:** aws4fetch client built with `service:'s3', region:'auto'`; presigned URLs carry explicit `X-Amz-Expires=600`; PUT content-type signed; copy response checked (`.ok`) AND final object existence verified via binding head before commit; **staging deleted only AFTER DB completion succeeds** (DB failure → 503, staging kept, retry-safe); stable final key `final/{user}/{media_object_id}` makes retries overwrite, no orphan accumulation; pending-object delete returns `cancelled_pending`, never a false "deleted".

## 19. Checkpoint — static-review fixes batch 4 (2026-10-23, after 869536b)
1. **Expired-staging cleanup:** `cloud_list_abandoned_staging()` (pending reservations past expiry with staging object still present) + `cloud_confirm_abandon()` (expire reservation + drop pending media row) called by the Worker ONLY after the R2 delete succeeds; failures keep the row listed for retry. **Accounting fix:** reserved usage now counts pending reservations whose media object still exists (`mo.status='pending'`) regardless of expiry, so expired-but-still-stored staging keeps consuming quota (no early release); the 3-outstanding limit still uses the unexpired filter.
2. **Replay byte_count:** idempotent /complete returns the selected `byte_count` from `media_objects` (was hardcoded 0); select columns extended; test asserts 74123.
3. **Reservation idempotency:** token reuse now checks status FIRST — pending → idempotent return with kind/mime/bytes **and clip_id** compared (NULL-safe `is distinct from`); completed → explicit `RESERVE: client_token already completed; use a new client_token`; expired/cancelled → same explicit rejection (unique (user_id, client_token) forbids reuse); Worker maps these to **409** (test added).
4. **SQL permissions:** explicit `grant execute ... to service_role` for all 10 `cloud_*` functions; revokes from PUBLIC/anon/authenticated retained (Postgres grants function EXECUTE to PUBLIC by default, so revokes are load-bearing); permission-verification query appended to the migration with expected proacl shape — **NOT verified: no database execution is authorized or has occurred.**

## 22. Checkpoint — global 10 GB cap hardening (2026-10-23)
**R2 fact (verified from official docs):** no native bucket storage cap exists ("Data storage per bucket: Unlimited" — developers.cloudflare.com/r2/platform/limits); 10 GB-month is a billing allowance. Dispatch's cap is therefore APPLICATION-LEVEL over reserve/complete-tracked objects; bytes written outside the contract (e.g., a stolen presigned URL within its 600 s life) are bounded by signed content-length + complete-time actual-size verification but cannot be made physically impossible.
**Implemented (migration `20261023000003_global_cap_hardening.sql` + Worker v8085e13d):** (1) usage now counts the PROMOTION DUPLICATE — an active object whose staging copy is unconfirmed counts twice (final+staging coexist), so concurrent promotions cannot overspend; (2) `cloud_complete_upload` keeps `staging_key`, atomically re-checks the projected post-promotion footprint under the same global advisory lock, raises `COMPLETE: global cap exceeded` past 10,000,000,000; (3) `cloud_confirm_staging_removed` releases the duplicate only after the Worker's R2 delete succeeds; `cloud_list_promotion_leftovers` + `/cleanup` reap crash leftovers idempotently (conservative: doubly counted until reaped); (4) presigned PUTs now sign `content-length = expected_bytes` so R2 rejects larger-than-reserved bodies; complete-time actual≤expected remains the authoritative check; abandoned uploads keep holding budget until R2 deletion is confirmed (0002 behavior, unchanged). No per-user Cloudflare limits added; app/db rules otherwise unchanged.
**Tests:** worker `node --test` → **24/24** (confirm-after-delete ordering, leftover reaping, length-presign); tsc clean; python cloud **22/22**; `git diff --check` clean. **Live:** Worker redeployed (v8085e13d), `/health` OK, `/cleanup` 200 processed:0 (gracefully handles the not-yet-applied 0003 function). **0003 NOT applied live** — `db push --linked` now fails 403 ("login role"): the CLI token reverted to the old Sappionapp account (Dispatch project no longer listed; window-login session didn't persist). **Owner action to finish:** in the Dispatch account dashboard → Account Settings → Access Tokens → create one → paste into `.env` after `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN=` (placeholder present; never paste in chat), or paste the database URI into `SUPABASE_DB_URL=`; then next agent runs `npx supabase db push --linked` (or `--db-url`) and re-verifies.

## 24. Checkpoint — LIVE end-to-end upload verification (2026-10-24) ✅
R2 S3 token created by owner (dashboard, Object Read & Write scoped to `dispatch-clips`), pasted into `.env`, set as Worker secrets (4 secrets total). **Live smoke test against production Worker + Supabase + R2 with synthetic users/files (smoke-test@dispatch.local, smoke-test-2@dispatch.local; 64-byte dummy thumbnails; no real media):**
- reserve → presigned PUT (real R2, HTTP 200) → complete (**byte_count 64 verified from the real object**) → **idempotent replay (byte_count 64, idempotent true)** → signed GET returned **exactly 64 bytes** → delete → `{"status":"deleted"}`.
- Cross-user isolation: user 2's own reserve OK; **user 2 completing user 1's reservation → HTTP 403**.
- Same client_token re-reserve returned the SAME pending reservation (idempotency, verified live incidentally).
- `/cleanup` 200 processed:0.
**Live finding (important):** R2 does **NOT** enforce signed content-length on presigned PUTs — a 65-byte body against a 64-byte-signed URL was ACCEPTED (HTTP 200). Code/test comments corrected; the signed header stays as defense-in-depth. **The authoritative oversized-upload guard is complete-time actual-size verification, which was verified live: the poisoned reservation completed with HTTP 422 "actual size exceeds reserved expectation" and was cleaned up.** Consequence for the 10 GB cap: physical transient overshoot is bounded by completed-time rejection — an abandoned oversized staging object holds its RESERVED bytes (expected_bytes, not actual) until cleanup reaps it, so accounting stays ≤ cap; truly oversized bytes beyond expectation exist transiently on R2 until rejection/cleanup and are outside the application cap (documented limitation, application-level cap).
Checks: worker tests 24/24, tsc clean, `git diff --check` clean. Remaining owner items: two-user RLS checklist + permission query in SQL editor; cron trigger for /cleanup (wrangler.toml has no triggers yet — next batch); PC sync client (Phase E) not started.

- **End-to-end remote flow now:** friend's phone (any network) → Supabase auth → submit URL via Worker → owner's PC claims and processes remotely → clips render on the PC. Friends' own-phone uploads to YouTube use the on-device OAuth vault.
- **Remaining for full remote review/publish:** clips metadata cloud sync + per-user publishing via vault tokens (currently clips render on the PC; review via owner's dashboard/admin). Physical phone steps remain owner-only (install debug APK, sign in, connect YouTube, record → submit → verify remote processing end-to-end on the POCO C65).

## 36. Release v1.1.0-alpha PUBLISHED (2026-10-24)
- Source: `main` `3658175` (sign-in gate + on-device OAuth + remote jobs shipped). Version bumped to **versionCode 4 / versionName 1.1.0** (gradle) so it installs over v1.0.2-alpha (code 3) and any earlier install.
- Built via hardened `prepare_release.ps1`: 28 Android unit tests passed, candidate identity verified (`com.resolvia.dispatch` v4/1.1.0, signer = trusted fingerprint `94f778…3804` — same key as v1.0.2-alpha, in-place update safe), `update.json` (sha256 `63f6270a…4669`, 11,553,485 B) generated from the actual APK.
- **Published:** GitHub release `v1.1.0-alpha` with Dispatch.apk + update.json; stable URL https://github.com/Resolviaai/Dispatch/releases/latest/download/Dispatch.apk now serves the new APK (downloaded byte-hash verified `63f6270ace88…`). Landing page updated to 1.1.0 and redeployed to gh-pages.
- Version bump commit: push next session or with final commit. Device install/verification remains owner-side.

## 37. Security audit + fix (2026-10-24, resume from 80545ff)
- Checkpoint verified: HEAD == origin/main == `80545ff`, clean, no later commits; college-PC work ignored per owner.
- **Live audit finding (priority 1):** migrations 0004/0006/0007 created `staging_watch`, `oauth_states`, `youtube_connections`, `processing_jobs` WITHOUT explicit revokes — Postgres default left anon/authenticated with INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/**TRUNCATE** (TRUNCATE bypasses RLS). RLS row policies were blocking row writes, but this violated least privilege and one policy mistake from exploitability.
- **Fix applied LIVE:** migration `20261024000008_revoke_default_grants.sql` (all four revoked from anon+authenticated; `processing_jobs` retains RLS-scoped SELECT for the app's own-jobs view). Post-fix live verification: only SELECT grants remain for authenticated across ALL public tables; adversarial REST probes return **42501 permission denied** on all sensitive tables (tokens/OAuth states/queue invisible and un-writable via client API). `on_auth_user_created` trigger confirmed present (profiles auto-create works).
- **Regression guard:** `tests/test_migration_grant_hygiene.py` — every `create table public.*` must be covered by a revoke (per-table or schema-wide) or the migration fails CI-style checks; also rejects any non-SELECT grant to anon/authenticated. Caught+fixed its own first run (0001 uses schema-wide revoke form).
- **Flaky test fixed:** vault tamper test now corrupts a middle byte deterministically (base64-tail edits decode to identical bytes ~40% of runs).
- Priorities 2–5 re-confirmed unchanged: quota contract live-tested (24/24 worker tests incl. concurrency/duplicate/expiry), Worker auth unchanged, R2 signed-URL lifecycle verified in prior live runs.
- **Tests:** combined scoped suite **42/42 × 5 consecutive runs** (hygiene guard + cloud config/quota + auth session + credential removal + privacy gate + vault/remote jobs); worker 24/24 + tsc clean; `git diff --check` clean; no secrets in diff.

# Dispatch UI/UX & Mobile Engineering Invariants

> **Scope:** Repository-wide frontend constitution (Web Review Studio & Android Native Jetpack Compose).  
> **Source Specification:** `c:\CODE\Dispatch\DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`  
> **Curriculum Baseline:** Forensic synthesis of 10 Mobile UI/UX Video Masterclasses (`design_research/`) + LeadMiner Design Tokens + CapCut Interaction Model.

Every agent working on frontend code, layout templates, components, and Android Compose views in this repository must strictly adhere to the following architectural rules:

---

## 1. The Single-Direction Law (Mobile Viewports)
* On mobile viewports and mobile frames (`isMobileFrame` or phone screens), every screen section moves along strictly **ONE** physical axis: either a vertical card stack OR a horizontal snap card carousel.
* Never lay out 2D multi-column desktop grids on mobile screens. Desktop 2-column or 12-column layouts must collapse to clean, unconstrained 1-column vertical stacks on mobile.

## 2. Biomechanical 44px Hitboxes & Safe Areas
* Every interactive element (button, icon trigger, tab, chip, slider thumb) must maintain a minimum touch bounding box of **$44\text{px} \times 44\text{px}$** (matching the human thumb contact patch).
* Maintain at least $8\text{px}$ separation between adjacent touch hitboxes to prevent mistaps.
* Bottom navigation bars and floating controls must respect the modern hardware safe area, clearing the $34\text{px}$ home indicator bar with dedicated bottom spacing.

## 3. The 4-Layer Elevation Architecture (60/30/10 Rule)
Never pick arbitrary hex colors or AI neon palettes. Every surface belongs to one of four calibrated layers:
* **Layer 0 (Canvas Root - 60%):** `#161616` (Deep charcoal studio anchor, `bg-studio`, `bg-background`).
* **Layer 1 (Cards & Elevated Surfaces - 30%):** `#1C1C1C` with border `#2E2E2E` (`bg-surface-100`, `border-border`).
* **Layer 2 (Recessed Wells & Inputs):** `#232323` (`bg-surface-200`) with deeper slider tracks and wells at `#282828` (`bg-surface-300`).
* **Layer 3 (Hero Brand CTA & Focus - 10%):** Electric Creator Blue `#2563EB` (`bg-primary`, hover `#3B82F6`, soft glow `rgba(37,99,235,0.15)`).
* **Semantic Colors (Functional Status Only):** Success `#22C55E`, Warning `#F59E0B`, Danger `#EF4444`. Never use semantic colors for decorative backgrounds.

## 4. The Law of the Single Hero CTA
* Each viewport must have **exactly ONE primary glowing hero action button** (`bg-primary text-white shadow-hero-glow`).
* Secondary and destructive actions ("Reject", "Cancel", "Back") must remain neutral recessed surfaces (`bg-surface-200 border-border text-text-main hover:bg-surface-300`). Never place competing brightly-colored buttons side-by-side.

## 5. Senior Typographic Limit & Numeric Place-Value Alignment
* Operational dashboard and studio screens must use **maximum 4 font sizes** ($18\text{px}$, $14\text{px}$, $12\text{px}$, $10\text{px}$) and **2 font weights** (Semibold for titles/headers, Regular/Medium for body/labels).
* **Numeric Place-Value Rule:** All numbers, durations, timestamps, speeds, file sizes, and percentages must strictly enforce `font-mono tabular-nums`. In tables and lists, numbers must be **right-aligned** so digits line up by place value.
* Always use non-breaking spaces before units (`42&nbsp;s`, `18.4&nbsp;MB`). Use the typographic ellipsis glyph `…` (`&hellip;`) rather than three periods `...`.

## 6. Concentric Geometry
* Nested elements inside rounded cards must follow exact concentric curvature mathematics:
  $$R_{\text{inner}} = R_{\text{outer}} - \text{Padding}$$
  Avoid mismatched pill vs squircle radiuses that create uneven visual gaps.

## 7. Anti-AI Vibecoding Standards (Prohibited Patterns)
* **Zero Native OS Emojis:** Never use emojis (🚀, 💡, 🔥, ⚡, 📦) as interface icons. Always use crisp SVG vector icons (`lucide-react`, Phosphor) with uniform $1.5\text{px}\text{--}2.0\text{px}$ stroke widths.
* **No Harsh Drop Shadows:** Never use opaque black drop shadows (`rgba(0,0,0,0.8)`). Use subtle border elevation (`border-border` `#2E2E2E`) paired with soft ambient diffusion.
* **No Multi-Color Neon Gradients:** Never apply blue-to-green or purple-to-cyan gradients to buttons or card backgrounds.

## 8. Tactile Feedback & Micro-Interactions
* Every clickable button and interactive card must provide immediate tactile feedback on touch/click:
  `active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-150`.
* All states must be fully specified: default, hover, active (pressed), disabled, and loading.

import java.util.Properties

plugins {
    id("com.android.application")
    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.android")
    id("com.google.devtools.ksp")
    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.compose")
}

android {
    namespace = "com.resolvia.dispatch"
    compileSdk = 36

    defaultConfig {
        applicationId = "com.resolvia.dispatch"
        minSdk = 26
        targetSdk = 34
        versionCode = 4
        versionName = "1.1.0"

        testInstrumentationRunner = "androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner"
        vectorDrawables {
            useSupportLibrary = true
        }
    }

    signingConfigs {
        getByName("debug") {
            enableV1Signing = true
            enableV2Signing = true
        }
        // Release signing from a git-ignored keystore.properties (see OWNER setup);
        // if absent, assembleRelease produces an unsigned APK and the CI/release
        // pipeline must not publish it.
        val keystoreProps = rootProject.file("keystore.properties")
        if (keystoreProps.exists()) {
            create("release") {
                val props = Properties().apply {
                    keystoreProps.inputStream().use { load(it) }
                }
                storeFile = rootProject.file(props.getProperty("storeFile"))
                storePassword = props.getProperty("storePassword")
                keyAlias = props.getProperty("keyAlias")
                keyPassword = props.getProperty("keyPassword")
                enableV1Signing = true
                enableV2Signing = true
            }
        }
    }

    buildTypes {
        debug {
            signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("debug")
        }
        release {
            if (signingConfigs.findByName("release") != null) {
                signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("release")
            }
            isMinifyEnabled = false
            proguardFiles(
                getDefaultProguardFile("proguard-android-optimize.txt"),
                "proguard-rules.pro"
            )
        }
    }
    compileOptions {
        sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
        targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    }
    kotlinOptions {
        jvmTarget = "17"
    }
    buildFeatures {
        compose = true
    }
   }

dependencies {
    implementation("androidx.browser:browser:1.8.0")
    // JVM unit tests: real org.json (Android's is a stub that throws "not mocked")

FILE: android\app\src\main\java\com\resolvia\dispatch\bridge\DispatchNativeBridge.kt
